--require-plan-id --require-traceability --json\\n```\\n\\nRun broader validation only after implementation discovers the repo's current\\nwrapper expectations and confirms the changed files.\\n\\n## Rollback Plan\\n\\n- Revert the `repo doctor` parser route and implementation helper.\\n- Leave existing `repo doctor-catalog`, `repo surface`, and `skills proof`\\n  untouched.\\n- Remove only tests and docs added for `repo doctor`.\\n- If catalog parity is fixed as a prerequisite, keep that fix unless it is\\n  proven incorrect, because it repairs a live blocker outside the new command.\\n\\n## Follow-On Work\\n\\n### F1: `ask skills improve \\\"<goal>\\\"`\\n\\nRoute a user goal to the best capability, using existing goal routing and\\ncatalog parity checks. Output one recommended skill or handle, why it matched,\\nreachability status, proof status, and one next command.\\n\\n### F2: `ask skills explain <skill-or-handle>`\\n\\nReturn concise capability guidance: what it is for, when to use it, canonical\\nsource path, runtime projection path, command handles, required validation, and\\nknown limitations.\\n\\n### F3: `ask skills prove <skill-or-goal>`\\n\\nAdd `prove` as the user-facing verb while preserving `proof` compatibility.\\nSeparate reachability proof, structural audit proof, and outcome proof.\\n\\n### F4: Agent Skills Kit Analytics Projection\\n\\nUse expanded Codex skill analytics as evidence input. Agent Skills Kit should\\nown ASK-local projections and proof decisions, not raw telemetry ingest.\\n\\nCandidate projection:\\n\\n```text\\n.skill-telemetry/skill-invocations.jsonl\\n.skill-telemetry/skill-scorecards/*.json\\n```\\n\\nProjection fields should include `skill_id`, `plugin_id`, `turn_id_hash`,\\n`thread_id_hash`, `invoke_type`, `scope`, `model_slug`,\\n`product_client_id_hash`, timestamp, and privacy-safe repository attribution\\nwhen available.\\n\\nFirst implementation slice:\\n\\n- Add an ASK-local reader for `.skill-telemetry/skill-invocations.jsonl`.\\n- Wire `ask skills prove <skill-or-goal>` to report analytics status from that\\n  projection when present.\\n- Keep missing analytics honest as `unavailable_or_legacy`; do not treat\\n  analytics as structural validation or outcome proof.\\n- Keep raw telemetry parsing out of Agent Skills Kit. Session collector remains\\n  the owner of raw source normalization in F5.\\n\\n### F5: Session Collector Changes\\n\\nUpdate `$HOME/.agents/session-collector` to normalize supported\\nskill invocation events from:\\n\\n- `~/.agents/otel-collector/data/raw/*.ndjson`;\\n- `~/.codex/sessions`.\\n\\nAdd these new artifact files to the existing bundle writer and manifest:\\n\\n- `skill-invocations.json`;\\n- `skill-invocation-summary.json`;\\n- `skill-proof-candidates.json`.\\n\\nKeep these existing bundle artifacts stable for current downstream consumers:\\n\\n- `aggregate.json`;\\n- `index.json`;\\n- `skillify-candidates.json`;\\n- `skill-refactor-evidence.json`;\\n- `harness-engineering-evidence.json`;\\n- `insight-evidence-extension.json`;\\n- `solved-problems.json`;\\n- `redaction-report.json`;\\n- `manifest.json`.\\n\\nThe collector should remove these roots from the default skill-analytics source\\npath:\\n\\n- `~/.codex-red/sessions`;\\n- `~/.codex-main/sessions`;\\n- optional Claude session metadata.\\n\\nIf backward compatibility requires retaining old CLI flags, keep them explicit\\nopt-in, mark them deprecated in help text, and prove they do not affect the\\ndefault skill-analytics output.\\n\\nRequired normalized record semantics:\\n\\n- native Codex `skill_invocation` events are\\n  `evidence_class: reachability_attribution`;\\n- text-derived skill mentions remain `evidence_class: legacy_mention`;\\n- missing native analytics are reported as\\n  `analytics_status: unavailable_or_legacy`;\\n- raw thread IDs, turn IDs, repo URLs, account markers, prompt text, and email\\n  addresses are redacted or hashed before they reach bundle artifacts.\\n\\nValidation for this unit:\\n\\n```bash\\ncd $HOME/.agents/session-collector\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 ruff check .\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 python -m py_compile main.py evidence.py renderer.py tests/test_session_collector.py\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 python -m unittest discover -s tests -v\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 python main.py --days 7 --max-sessions 200 --codex-sessions-dir $HOME/.codex/sessions --output /tmp/session-collector-skill-analytics.json --bundle-dir /tmp/session-collector-skill-analytics-bundle --verbose\\n```\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- existing bundle filenames and the three new skill-invocation filenames are\\n  present in `manifest.json`;\\n- default source resolution includes OTel raw files and `~/.codex/sessions`;\\n- default source resolution excludes Codex Red, Codex Main, and Claude metadata;\\n- tests cover native analytics, legacy mentions, malformed records, duplicate\\n  records, missing `turn_id`, missing `plugin_id`, source-missing state, and\\n  stale artifact classification.\\n\\n### F6: OTel Collector Changes\\n\\nUpdate `$HOME/.agents/otel-collector` to preserve raw skill\\ninvocation attribution metadata and expose low-cardinality aggregate health:\\n\\n- seen skill invocation event count;\\n- seen plugin ID count;\\n- seen turn ID coverage;\\n- malformed or dropped event counts;\\n- source freshness.\\n\\nDo not expose high-cardinality per-thread or per-turn breakdowns in `/stats`.\\n\\n### F7: `ask repo closeout --changed`\\n\\nCompose changed-file awareness, validation recommendations, repo doctor state,\\nand required closeout checks into one completion-readiness command.\\n\\n### F8: README Golden Path Rewrite\\n\\nAfter the commands exist, rewrite the README first screen around:\\n\\n```bash\\n./bin/ask repo doctor --json --robot\\n./bin/ask skills improve \\\"<goal>\\\" --json --robot\\n./bin/ask skills explain <handle> --json --robot\\n./bin/ask skills prove <handle> --json --robot\\n./bin/ask repo closeout --changed --json --robot\\n```\\n\\nDo not publish this as the live first screen until the commands are implemented.\\n\\n## Technical Review\\n\\nReview status: passed with prior blockers repaired.\\n\\n### Finding TR-1: Spec Uses `prove`, Current CLI Uses `proof`\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `./bin/ask skills --help` shows `proof`.\\n- `Infrastructure/bin/ask` contains parser support for `proof`.\\n- No current `prove` route was found.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- First implementation must keep `proof` stable.\\n- `prove` is deferred to F3 as a compatibility addition or alias.\\n\\n### Finding TR-2: Harness Plan Gate Historical Debt\\n\\nSeverity: resolved.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `./Infrastructure/scripts/harness-cli.sh plan-gate --require-plan-id --require-traceability --json`\\n  previously failed with stale-plan and missing-`plan_id` findings in existing\\n  plans.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- Historical plans now carry `plan_id` frontmatter.\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/harness-cli.sh` now applies a repo-local default\\n  `--max-age` for `plan-gate` unless the caller supplies one explicitly.\\n- The gate now passes with zero findings.\\n\\n### Finding TR-3: Catalog Parity Drift\\n\\nSeverity: resolved.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot` previously reported canonical\\n  skill count `21` and README observed count `20`.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- README now reports `21` canonical skills.\\n- `./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot` passes with\\n  `decision_status: resolved`.\\n- Preserve a fixture that proves blocked behavior for future drift.\\n\\n### Finding TR-4: Path Casing Needs Exact Pathspecs\\n\\nSeverity: low.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `git ls-files` shows tracked plan and spec paths under canonical `Docs/**`.\\n- `git status --short -- Docs/plans Docs/specs` reports the new files under\\n  uppercase `Docs/**`; mixed uppercase/lowercase pathspecs can still echo the\\n  caller's lowercase spelling on macOS because `core.ignorecase` is true.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- Use exact uppercase `Docs/**` pathspecs for status, staging, and review.\\n- Do not create a second lowercase tree.\\n\\n### Finding TR-5: First Slice Must Avoid Three-Project Coupling\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- The spec correctly includes Agent Skills Kit, session-collector, and\\n  otel-collector, but the first command does not need collector changes.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- Keep collector changes in F5 and F6.\\n- Implement `repo doctor` first using existing local signals.\\n\\n### Finding TR-6: Collector Follow-On Must Preserve Existing Evidence Bundle\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- The live session collector already emits bundle artifacts consumed by\\n  `skillify`, `skill-refactor`, `insight-report`, and `harness-engineering`.\\n- The spec adds native skill-invocation artifacts, but those artifacts should\\n  extend the bundle rather than replacing it.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- F5 requires the existing bundle filenames to remain present.\\n- F5 requires `manifest.json` to list both the existing artifacts and the new\\n  skill-invocation artifacts.\\n\\n### Finding TR-7: Collector Source Defaults Conflict With The Narrow Feature Scope\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- Current collector defaults still include Codex Red and Codex Main rollout\\n  directories, and the CLI still exposes legacy Claude metadata flags.\\n- The skill-analytics source contract for this feature is intentionally limited\\n  to OTel raw files and `~/.codex/sessions`.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- F5 requires Codex Red, Codex Main, and Claude metadata to be removed from the\\n  default skill-analytics source path.\\n- Any retained compatibility flags must be explicit opt-in, deprecated in help\\n  text, and covered by tests proving they do not affect default output.\\n\\n## Risks and Blockers\\n\\n- Future catalog parity drift can block `skills improve` and broad goal routing.\\n- Future plan-gate failures should now indicate new debt, not inherited\\n  historical plan metadata.\\n- Mixed-case pathspecs can create confusing status output on macOS; use\\n  canonical `Docs/**` pathspecs.\\n- Shelling out from `ask` into `ask` would make doctor brittle and slow; extract\\n  helper functions instead.\\n- Surfacing thousands of repo-surface findings in doctor would reproduce the\\n  product problem this work is meant to solve.\\n- Replacing the session-collector bundle would break existing downstream\\n  evidence consumers; extend it instead.\\n- Leaving unsupported collector roots in the default skill-analytics path would\\n  reintroduce the exact source-scope ambiguity this spec is trying to remove.\\n\\n## Sources and References\\n\\n- `Docs/specs/2026-05-06-feat-agent-first-golden-path-product-compression-spec.md`\\n- there is no `repo doctor` route yet.\\n- `fd` evidence shows existing tests for `repo doctor-catalog`, `repo surface`,\\n  `repo status`, and the `ask` CLI, but no dedicated `repo doctor` test yet.\\n- `$HOME/.agents/session-collector` is the collector code project;\\n  its raw telemetry input root is\\n  `$HOME/.agents/otel-collector/data/raw`.\\n- A narrowed session-collector run using OTel raw files and\\n  `$HOME/.codex/sessions` wrote\\n  `/tmp/ask-session-collector-spec-evidence.json` and\\n  `/tmp/ask-session-collector-spec-bundle`.\\n- That collector run kept `391942` records across `596` source files:\\n  `391759` Codex rollout lines and `183` OTel log lines.\\n- Current collector output already includes bundle files consumed by downstream\\n  workflows: `aggregate.json`, `index.json`, `skillify-candidates.json`,\\n  `skill-refactor-evidence.json`, `harness-engineering-evidence.json`,\\n  `insight-evidence-extension.json`, `solved-problems.json`,\\n  `redaction-report.json`, and `manifest.json`.\\n- Current collector skill evidence is mention-derived, not native\\n  `skill_invocation` analytics. The sampled run reported `simplify: 140`,\\n  `skill-refactor: 2`, and `harness-engineering: 3287`.\\n\\n## Scope Boundaries\\n\\nIn scope for the first implementation:\\n\\n- `Infrastructure/bin/ask` parser and dispatch updates for `repo doctor`;\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/lib/ask/**` helper code for the golden-path envelope;\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/lib/ask/commands/repo.py` doctor composition logic;\\n- focused tests under `Infrastructure/tests/**`;\\n- concise docs that introduce `ask repo doctor` as the first agent command;\\n- focused command metadata or help text needed to expose `repo doctor`.\\n\\nOut of scope for the first implementation:\\n\\n- deleting historical repo-surface artifacts;\\n- changing generated skill projections;\\n- changing current `ask skills proof` behavior;\\n- implementing `ask skills improve`, `ask skills explain`, `ask skills prove`,\\n  or `ask repo closeout --changed`;\\n- modifying `$HOME/.agents/session-collector`;\\n- modifying `$HOME/.agents/otel-collector`;\\n- ingesting or storing high-cardinality per-turn analytics in Agent Skills Kit.\\n\\n## Key Decisions\\n\\n### D1: Start With `ask repo doctor`\\n\\n`ask repo doctor` is the smallest useful golden-path command because it answers\\nthe first question every coding agent has before touching the repo:\\n\\n```text\\nCan I work safely, and what should I do next?\\n```\\n\\nThis command can be implemented by composing existing repo signals. It does not\\nrequire new skill semantics or collector ingestion.\\n\\n### D2: Add A Shared Golden-Path Envelope\\n\\nThe first command should establish a reusable response shape:\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"agent_summary\\\": \\\"Usable, but blocked by catalog parity drift.\\\",\\n  \\\"blocking\\\": true,\\n  \\\"blockers\\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\\"id\\\": \\\"catalog_parity\\\",\\n      \\\"severity\\\": \\\"blocker\\\",\\n      \\\"summary\\\": \\\"Canonical skill count is 21 but README reports 20.\\\",\\n      \\\"next_command\\\": \\\"./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot\\\"\\n    }\\n  ],\\n  \\\"next_command\\\": \\\"./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot\\\",\\n  \\\"signals\\\": {},\\n  \\\"diagnostic_debt\\\": []\\n}\\n```\\n\\nExisting `--json` and `--robot` wrappers should continue to use the repo's\\nstandard result envelope. The golden-path fields should live inside the command\\npayload rather than replacing lower-level diagnostics.\\n\\n### D3: Compose Existing Helpers, Do Not Shell Out\\n\\nThe implementation should call existing Python helpers or command functions\\nwhere possible. It should not invoke `./bin/ask` subprocesses and parse their\\nstdout from inside the `ask` implementation.\\n\\nIf a lower-level command currently only exposes CLI-oriented behavior, extract a\\nsmall pure helper before composing it into doctor.\\n\\n### D4: Keep Current `skills proof` Stable\\n\\nThe current CLI already has `ask skills proof`. The spec's desired\\n`ask skills prove` command should be handled in a later compatibility unit.\\nThe first slice must not rename, remove, or silently change the existing\\n`proof` command.\\n\\n### D5: Preserve Catalog Parity As A First-Class Blocker\\n\\nCatalog parity drift has been repaired in the current tree, but it remains the\\nfirst-class drift condition that blocks broad goal routing. The new doctor\\ncommand should surface any future catalog parity drift as a blocker with one\\nclear next command.\\n\\nImplementation may fix catalog parity before adding doctor, but the doctor tests\\nshould still include a fixture that proves catalog parity drift is presented as\\na blocking signal.\\n\\n### D6: Treat Runtime Budget Differently In Default And Strict Modes\\n\\nRuntime budget over advisory threshold should be a warning by default and a\\nblocker in strict mode, unless the lower-level runtime budget check already\\nreports policy violations.\\n\\nThe command should support a strict flag only if it matches existing `ask`\\nstyle. If no shared strict pattern exists, strict behavior can be deferred.\\n\\n## Task Graph\\n\\n```yaml\\ntasks:\\n  - id: P0\\n    title: Confirm repaired baseline\\n    depends_on: []\\n  - id: P1\\n    title: Create response contract\\n    depends_on: [P0]\\n  - id: P2\\n    title: Expose command route\\n    depends_on: [P1]\\n  - id: P3\\n    title: Compose repo signals\\n    depends_on: [P2]\\n  - id: P4\\n    title: Prove behavior with tests\\n    depends_on: [P3]\\n  - id: P5\\n    title: Document new entrypoint\\n    depends_on: [P4]\\n  - id: P6\\n    title: Close traceability evidence\\n    depends_on: [P5]\\n```\\n\\n```text\\nP0 -> P1 -> P2 -> P3 -> P4 -> P5 -> P6\\n```\\n\\n- P0 confirms the repaired baseline.\\n- P1 creates the response contract.\\n- P2 exposes the command route.\\n- P3 composes repo signals.\\n- P4 proves the behavior with tests.\\n- P5 documents the new entrypoint.\\n- P6 runs validation and writes handoff evidence.\\n\\nFollow-on units F1-F8 depend on P6 unless explicitly split into separate\\ntracked implementation work.\\n\\n## Implementation Units\\n\\n### P0: Handoff Cleanup And Baseline Confirmation\\n\\nOwner: implementation agent.\\n\\nActions:\\n\\n- Confirm current branch and uncommitted files.\\n- Confirm new spec and plan files are reported under canonical `Docs/**` paths\\n  by using exact uppercase pathspecs.\\n- Rerun `./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot`.\\n- Preserve a catalog parity drift fixture even though the live README/catalog\\n  drift has been repaired.\\n- Add a Linear workpad comment linking JSC-246, the product-compression spec,\\n  and this plan.\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- implementation branch is known;\\n- unrelated dirty files are identified and left untouched;\\n- catalog parity pass state is recorded with exact command output;\\n- Linear has a workpad note or the lack of Linear write access is recorded.\\n\\n### P1: Shared Golden-Path Envelope\\n\\nOwner: `Infrastructure/scripts/lib/ask/**`.\\n\\nActions:\\n\\n- Add a small helper for agent-facing command summaries.\\n- Keep fields stable: `agent_summary`, `blocking`, `blockers`,\\n  `next_command`, `signals`, `diagnostic_debt`.\\n- Make blockers deterministic and sorted by severity, then stable ID.\\n- Use the state vocabulary from the spec: `pass`, `warn`, `block`, `error`,\\n  `unknown`, and `skipped` for `signals.<name>.state`.\\n- Require `blocking: true` to include at least one `severity: blocker` entry.\\n- Return exactly one primary `next_command`; put alternates in\\n  `metadata.next_steps` or signal-specific detail.\\n- Support concise human output and full JSON output through existing result\\n  conventions.\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- helper is unit-testable without command-line parsing;\\n- at least one test covers blocker ordering and primary next-command selection.\\n\\n### P2: `ask repo doctor` Parser And Dispatch\\n\\nOwner: `Infrastructure/bin/ask`.\\n\\nActions:\\n\\n- Add `doctor` to the `repo` subparser.\\n- Wire dispatch to a `repo_doctor` implementation.\\n- Preserve current `doctor-catalog`, `surface`, `status`, and `validate`\\n  behavior.\\n- Update help text so `repo doctor` reads as the agent health entrypoint.\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- `./bin/ask repo doctor --help` works;\\n- invalid command correction behavior remains unchanged for nearby commands.\\n\\n### P3: Doctor Signal Composition\\n\\nOwner: `Infrastructure/scripts/lib/ask/commands/repo.py` and extracted helpers.\\n\\nActions:\\n\\n- Compose these signals:\\n  - repo status and sync state;\\n  - catalog parity via the same logic as `doctor-catalog`;\\n  - runtime budget health;\\n  - generated command handle health;\\n  - repo surface status and diagnostic debt.\\n- Extract pure helper functions for any signal that currently exists only as a\\n  CLI-oriented command path; do not call `./bin/ask` from inside `ask`.\\n- Map signals into blocker, warning, or informational classes.\\n- Pick one primary `next_command`.\\n- Include enough signal detail for robot callers to act without reading lower\\n  level reports first.\\n\\nDefault severity rules:\\n\\n- catalog parity drift: blocker;\\n- unresolved handle collision or handle check failure: blocker;\\n- runtime budget policy violation: blocker;\\n- runtime budget advisory threshold only: warning;\\n- repo surface warning: warning unless strict mode is implemented and enabled;\\n- repo status failure: blocker;\\n- synced skills: informational pass.\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- current live repo returns a compact summary;\\n- catalog parity drift, if still present, is the primary blocker;\\n- surface debt is present as diagnostic debt rather than hiding the catalog\\n  blocker.\\n- each signal includes `state`, `summary`, `source`, and, when actionable,\\n  `next_command`.\\n\\n### P4: Focused Tests And Fixtures\\n\\nOwner: `Infrastructure/tests/**`.\\n\\nActions:\\n\\n- Add tests for:\\n  - parser route exists;\\n  - JSON payload includes golden-path fields;\\n  - catalog parity drift blocks and selects the catalog doctor next command;\\n  - non-blocking surface warning appears as diagnostic debt;\\n  - all-pass fixture returns `blocking: false` and selects the existing\\n    `./bin/ask repo status --json --robot` inspection command as\\n    `next_command` until `skills improve` or `repo closeout --changed` exists;\\n  - help output includes `repo doctor`.\\n- Reuse existing fixture style from `test_ask_repo_doctor_catalog.py` and\\n  `test_ask_repo_surface.py`.\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- focused tests pass locally;\\n- no unrelated broad fixture churn.\\n\\n### P5: Minimal Docs Front Door\\n\\nOwner: README and focused docs only.\\n\\nActions:\\n\\n- Add a concise golden-path snippet:\\n\\n```bash\\n./bin/ask repo doctor --json --robot\\n./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot\\n./bin/ask repo surface --json --robot\\n```\\n\\n- Avoid broad README rewrite in this first PR.\\n- Link to the spec and this plan from any implementation note.\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- docs describe `repo doctor` as the first agent command;\\n- docs do not claim `skills improve`, `skills explain`, `skills prove`, or\\n  `repo closeout --changed` are implemented until they exist.\\n\\n### P6: Validation And Handoff\\n\\nOwner: implementation agent.\\n\\nActions:\\n\\n- Run focused tests for the new command.\\n- Run the repo's relevant validation wrapper.\\n- Rerun harness plan-gate and record the pass/fail state.\\n- Capture exact command outcomes in the final handoff and Linear workpad.\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- new command behavior is validated or blockers are explicit;\\n- no new plan-gate issue is introduced by this plan;\\n- final handoff records pass, fail, and blocked results with exact commands.\\n\\n## First-Slice Technical Design\\n\\n### Command Shape\\n\\n```bash\\n./bin/ask repo doctor\\n./bin/ask repo doctor --json\\n./bin/ask repo doctor --json --robot\\n```\\n\\nOptional flags may include `--strict` only if the existing parser has a\\ncompatible pattern.\\n\\n### JSON Payload\\n\\nThe command payload should use the existing `ask` result envelope and include a\\ndoctor object shaped like this:\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\\"repo_doctor\\\": {\\n    \\\"agent_summary\\\": \\\"Usable, but blocked by catalog parity drift.\\\",\\n    \\\"blocking\\\": true,\\n    \\\"blockers\\\": [\\n      {\\n        \\\"id\\\": \\\"catalog_parity\\\",\\n        \\\"severity\\\": \\\"blocker\\\",\\n        \\\"summary\\\": \\\"Canonical skill count is 21 but README reports 20.\\\",\\n        \\\"next_command\\\": \\\"./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot\\\",\\n        \\\"source\\\": \\\"repo.doctor_catalog\\\"\\n      }\\n    ],\\n    \\\"next_command\\\": \\\"./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot\\\",\\n    \\\"signals\\\": {\\n      \\\"repo_status\\\": { \\\"state\\\": \\\"pass\\\" },\\n      \\\"catalog_parity\\\": { \\\"state\\\": \\\"block\\\" },\\n      \\\"runtime_budget\\\": { \\\"state\\\": \\\"warn\\\" },\\n      \\\"handles\\\": { \\\"state\\\": \\\"pass\\\" },\\n      \\\"surface\\\": { \\\"state\\\": \\\"warn\\\" }\\n    },\\n    \\\"diagnostic_debt\\\": [\\n      {\\n        \\\"id\\\": \\\"repo_surface_findings\\\",\\n        \\\"severity\\\": \\\"warning\\\",\\n        \\\"summary\\\": \\\"Repo surface inventory has historical blocking findings.\\\"\\n      }\\n    ]\\n  }\\n}\\n```\\n\\nExact nesting can adapt to existing command conventions, but the field names\\nabove are the first-slice contract.\\n\\n### Human Output\\n\\nHuman output should prefer compact action language:\\n\\n```text\\nRepo doctor: blocked\\nSummary: Usable, but blocked by catalog parity drift.\\nNext: ./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot\\n```\\n\\nDo not print thousands of repo-surface findings in the first screen.\\n\\n### Integration Points\\n\\nLikely files:\\n\\n- `Infrastructure/bin/ask`\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/lib/ask/commands/repo.py`\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/lib/ask/commands/runtime.py`, if runtime budget logic\\n  needs extraction\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/lib/ask/commands/skills.py`, if handles check logic\\n  needs extraction\\n- `Infrastructure/tests/test_ask_cli.py`\\n- `Infrastructure/tests/test_ask_repo_doctor.py`\\n\\nImplementation should verify exact helper boundaries before editing.\\n\\n## Acceptance and Traceability\\n\\n| Acceptance ID | Requirement                                                                                                                               | Evidence                                       |\\n| ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |       | CLI help output                                |\\n| AC2           | `./bin/ask repo doctor --json --robot` returns `agent_summary`, `blocking`, `blockers`, `next_command`, `signals`, and `diagnostic_debt`. | focused test and live command                  |\\n| AC3           | Catalog parity drift is reported as a blocker.                                                                                            | fixture test and live command if drift remains |\\n| AC4           | Repo surface warning is summarized without flooding the first response.                                                                   | fixture test                                   |\\n| AC5           | Existing `repo doctor-catalog`, `repo surface`, and `skills proof` behavior remains compatible.                                           | regression tests                               |\\n| AC6           | The README or command docs point agents at `repo doctor` first.                                                                           | docs diff                                      |\\n| AC7           | Harness plan-gate state is recorded and this new plan carries `plan_id`.                                                                  | plan-gate output                               |\\n\\n## Validation Plan\\n\\nPlanning validation:\\n\\n```bash\\npython3 Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/docs_lint.py --mode warn --config Infrastructure/docs-policy.json --patterns '**/*.md' '**/*.mdx'\\n./Infrastructure/scripts/harness-cli.sh plan-gate --require-plan-id --require-traceability --json\\ngit status --short -- Docs/specs Docs/plans\\n```\\n\\nFirst implementation validation:\\n\\n```bash\\n./bin/ask repo doctor --help\\n./bin/ask repo doctor --json --robot\\npython3 -m pytest Infrastructure/tests/test_ask_repo_doctor.py Infrastructure/tests/test_ask_cli.py\\n./bin/ask repo validate --json --robot\\npython3 Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/docs_lint.py --mode warn --config Infrastructure/docs-policy.json --patterns '**/*.md' '**/*.mdx'\\n./Infrastructure/scripts/harness-cli.sh plan-gate --require-plan-id --require-traceability --json\\n```\\n\\nRun broader validation only after implementation discovers the repo's current\\nwrapper expectations and confirms the changed files.\\n\\n## Rollback Plan\\n\\n- Revert the `repo doctor` parser route and implementation helper.\\n- Leave existing `repo doctor-catalog`, `repo surface`, and `skills proof`\\n  untouched.\\n- Remove only tests and docs added for `repo doctor`.\\n- If catalog parity is fixed as a prerequisite, keep that fix unless it is\\n  proven incorrect, because it repairs a live blocker outside the new command.\\n\\n## Follow-On Work\\n\\n### F1: `ask skills improve \\\"<goal>\\\"`\\n\\nRoute a user goal to the best capability, using existing goal routing and\\ncatalog parity checks. Output one recommended skill or handle, why it matched,\\nreachability status, proof status, and one next command.\\n\\n### F2: `ask skills explain <skill-or-handle>`\\n\\nReturn concise capability guidance: what it is for, when to use it, canonical\\nsource path, runtime projection path, command handles, required validation, and\\nknown limitations.\\n\\n### F3: `ask skills prove <skill-or-goal>`\\n\\nAdd `prove` as the user-facing verb while preserving `proof` compatibility.\\nSeparate reachability proof, structural audit proof, and outcome proof.\\n\\n### F4: Agent Skills Kit Analytics Projection\\n\\nUse expanded Codex skill analytics as evidence input. Agent Skills Kit should\\nown ASK-local projections and proof decisions, not raw telemetry ingest.\\n\\nCandidate projection:\\n\\n```text\\n.skill-telemetry/skill-invocations.jsonl\\n.skill-telemetry/skill-scorecards/*.json\\n```\\n\\nProjection fields should include `skill_id`, `plugin_id`, `turn_id_hash`,\\n`thread_id_hash`, `invoke_type`, `scope`, `model_slug`,\\n`product_client_id_hash`, timestamp, and privacy-safe repository attribution\\nwhen available.\\n\\nFirst implementation slice:\\n\\n- Add an ASK-local reader for `.skill-telemetry/skill-invocations.jsonl`.\\n- Wire `ask skills prove <skill-or-goal>` to report analytics status from that\\n  projection when present.\\n- Keep missing analytics honest as `unavailable_or_legacy`; do not treat\\n  analytics as structural validation or outcome proof.\\n- Keep raw telemetry parsing out of Agent Skills Kit. Session collector remains\\n  the owner of raw source normalization in F5.\\n\\n### F5: Session Collector Changes\\n\\nUpdate `$HOME/.agents/session-collector` to normalize supported\\nskill invocation events from:\\n\\n- `~/.agents/otel-collector/data/raw/*.ndjson`;\\n- `~/.codex/sessions`.\\n\\nAdd these new artifact files to the existing bundle writer and manifest:\\n\\n- `skill-invocations.json`;\\n- `skill-invocation-summary.json`;\\n- `skill-proof-candidates.json`.\\n\\nKeep these existing bundle artifacts stable for current downstream consumers:\\n\\n- `aggregate.json`;\\n- `index.json`;\\n- `skillify-candidates.json`;\\n- `skill-refactor-evidence.json`;\\n- `harness-engineering-evidence.json`;\\n- `insight-evidence-extension.json`;\\n- `solved-problems.json`;\\n- `redaction-report.json`;\\n- `manifest.json`.\\n\\nThe collector should remove these roots from the default skill-analytics source\\npath:\\n\\n- `~/.codex-red/sessions`;\\n- `~/.codex-main/sessions`;\\n- optional Claude session metadata.\\n\\nIf backward compatibility requires retaining old CLI flags, keep them explicit\\nopt-in, mark them deprecated in help text, and prove they do not affect the\\ndefault skill-analytics output.\\n\\nRequired normalized record semantics:\\n\\n- native Codex `skill_invocation` events are\\n  `evidence_class: reachability_attribution`;\\n- text-derived skill mentions remain `evidence_class: legacy_mention`;\\n- missing native analytics are reported as\\n  `analytics_status: unavailable_or_legacy`;\\n- raw thread IDs, turn IDs, repo URLs, account markers, prompt text, and email\\n  addresses are redacted or hashed before they reach bundle artifacts.\\n\\nValidation for this unit:\\n\\n```bash\\ncd $HOME/.agents/session-collector\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 ruff check .\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 python -m py_compile main.py evidence.py renderer.py tests/test_session_collector.py\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 python -m unittest discover -s tests -v\\nUV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/session-collector-uv-cache uv run --python 3.12 python main.py --days 7 --max-sessions 200 --codex-sessions-dir $HOME/.codex/sessions --output /tmp/session-collector-skill-analytics.json --bundle-dir /tmp/session-collector-skill-analytics-bundle --verbose\\n```\\n\\nExit criteria:\\n\\n- existing bundle filenames and the three new skill-invocation filenames are\\n  present in `manifest.json`;\\n- default source resolution includes OTel raw files and `~/.codex/sessions`;\\n- default source resolution excludes Codex Red, Codex Main, and Claude metadata;\\n- tests cover native analytics, legacy mentions, malformed records, duplicate\\n  records, missing `turn_id`, missing `plugin_id`, source-missing state, and\\n  stale artifact classification.\\n\\n### F6: OTel Collector Changes\\n\\nUpdate `$HOME/.agents/otel-collector` to preserve raw skill\\ninvocation attribution metadata and expose low-cardinality aggregate health:\\n\\n- seen skill invocation event count;\\n- seen plugin ID count;\\n- seen turn ID coverage;\\n- malformed or dropped event counts;\\n- source freshness.\\n\\nDo not expose high-cardinality per-thread or per-turn breakdowns in `/stats`.\\n\\n### F7: `ask repo closeout --changed`\\n\\nCompose changed-file awareness, validation recommendations, repo doctor state,\\nand required closeout checks into one completion-readiness command.\\n\\n### F8: README Golden Path Rewrite\\n\\nAfter the commands exist, rewrite the README first screen around:\\n\\n```bash\\n./bin/ask repo doctor --json --robot\\n./bin/ask skills improve \\\"<goal>\\\" --json --robot\\n./bin/ask skills explain <handle> --json --robot\\n./bin/ask skills prove <handle> --json --robot\\n./bin/ask repo closeout --changed --json --robot\\n```\\n\\nDo not publish this as the live first screen until the commands are implemented.\\n\\n## Technical Review\\n\\nReview status: passed with prior blockers repaired.\\n\\n### Finding TR-1: Spec Uses `prove`, Current CLI Uses `proof`\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `./bin/ask skills --help` shows `proof`.\\n- `Infrastructure/bin/ask` contains parser support for `proof`.\\n- No current `prove` route was found.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- First implementation must keep `proof` stable.\\n- `prove` is deferred to F3 as a compatibility addition or alias.\\n\\n### Finding TR-2: Harness Plan Gate Historical Debt\\n\\nSeverity: resolved.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `./Infrastructure/scripts/harness-cli.sh plan-gate --require-plan-id --require-traceability --json`\\n  previously failed with stale-plan and missing-`plan_id` findings in existing\\n  plans.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- Historical plans now carry `plan_id` frontmatter.\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/harness-cli.sh` now applies a repo-local default\\n  `--max-age` for `plan-gate` unless the caller supplies one explicitly.\\n- The gate now passes with zero findings.\\n\\n### Finding TR-3: Catalog Parity Drift\\n\\nSeverity: resolved.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot` previously reported canonical\\n  skill count `21` and README observed count `20`.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- README now reports `21` canonical skills.\\n- `./bin/ask repo doctor-catalog --json --robot` passes with\\n  `decision_status: resolved`.\\n- Preserve a fixture that proves blocked behavior for future drift.\\n\\n### Finding TR-4: Path Casing Needs Exact Pathspecs\\n\\nSeverity: low.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- `git ls-files` shows tracked plan and spec paths under canonical `Docs/**`.\\n- `git status --short -- Docs/plans Docs/specs` reports the new files under\\n  uppercase `Docs/**`; mixed uppercase/lowercase pathspecs can still echo the\\n  caller's lowercase spelling on macOS because `core.ignorecase` is true.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- Use exact uppercase `Docs/**` pathspecs for status, staging, and review.\\n- Do not create a second lowercase tree.\\n\\n### Finding TR-5: First Slice Must Avoid Three-Project Coupling\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- The spec correctly includes Agent Skills Kit, session-collector, and\\n  otel-collector, but the first command does not need collector changes.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- Keep collector changes in F5 and F6.\\n- Implement `repo doctor` first using existing local signals.\\n\\n### Finding TR-6: Collector Follow-On Must Preserve Existing Evidence Bundle\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- The live session collector already emits bundle artifacts consumed by\\n  `skillify`, `skill-refactor`, `insight-report`, and `harness-engineering`.\\n- The spec adds native skill-invocation artifacts, but those artifacts should\\n  extend the bundle rather than replacing it.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- F5 requires the existing bundle filenames to remain present.\\n- F5 requires `manifest.json` to list both the existing artifacts and the new\\n  skill-invocation artifacts.\\n\\n### Finding TR-7: Collector Source Defaults Conflict With The Narrow Feature Scope\\n\\nSeverity: medium.\\n\\nEvidence:\\n\\n- Current collector defaults still include Codex Red and Codex Main rollout\\n  directories, and the CLI still exposes legacy Claude metadata flags.\\n- The skill-analytics source contract for this feature is intentionally limited\\n  to OTel raw files and `~/.codex/sessions`.\\n\\nResolution:\\n\\n- F5 requires Codex Red, Codex Main, and Claude metadata to be removed from the\\n  default skill-analytics source path.\\n- Any retained compatibility flags must be explicit opt-in, deprecated in help\\n  text, and covered by tests proving they do not affect default output.\\n\\n## Risks and Blockers\\n\\n- Future catalog parity drift can block `skills improve` and broad goal routing.\\n- Future plan-gate failures should now indicate new debt, not inherited\\n  historical plan metadata.\\n- Mixed-case pathspecs can create confusing status output on macOS; use\\n  canonical `Docs/**` pathspecs.\\n- Shelling out from `ask` into `ask` would make doctor brittle and slow; extract\\n  helper functions instead.\\n- Surfacing thousands of repo-surface findings in doctor would reproduce the\\n  product problem this work is meant to solve.\\n- Replacing the session-collector bundle would break existing downstream\\n  evidence consumers; extend it instead.\\n- Leaving unsupported collector roots in the default skill-analytics path would\\n  reintroduce the exact source-scope ambiguity this spec is one surface now blocks readiness claims.\\n- **Error propagation:** live-runner or scanner failures become explicit release blockers, not advisory notes.\\n- **State lifecycle risks:** incomplete harmonization can create contradictory guidance and misroutes; mitigated by P0 first.\\n- **API surface parity:** SKILL text, `agents/openai.yaml`, and family gates remain aligned as one contract surface.\\n- **Integration coverage:** cross-surface tests ensure docs and validators do not diverge after updates.\\n- **Rollback posture:** if live or security gates regress after partial merges, pause forward execution and revert the affected gate-level claim rather than downgrading acceptance semantics.\\n\\n## Risks and Dependencies\\n\\n- Runner instability can create false negatives in live mode.\\n  - Trigger: intermittent live-runner timeout or non-deterministic upstream failure.\\n  - Mitigation: retry-limited reruns plus explicit degraded-mode note; do not mark closeout complete without a successful trusted rerun.\\n- Security hardening can over-constrain legitimate workflows.\\n  - Trigger: scanner fixes reduce needed file or network behavior.\\n  - Mitigation: targeted allowlists, narrow safeguards, and documented compensating controls for any accepted residual risk.\\n- Quality-score tuning can unintentionally optimize for analyzer heuristics over real usefulness.\\n  - Trigger: score increases without clearer routing outcomes in realistic prompts.\\n  - Mitigation: human-reviewed realistic prompt checks remain mandatory before acceptance.\\n- Contract reconciliation may touch previously completed artifacts and create historical confusion.\\n  - Trigger: edits land in historical \\\"complete\\\" plans instead of active contract docs.\\n  - Mitigation: update active spec/reference surfaces only; preserve completed plans as historical evidence.\\n\\n## Documentation and Operational Notes\\n\\n- Use this plan as the canonical execution input for all remaining family \\\"gold standard\\\" work.\\n- Keep closeout evidence in a single run summary artifact linked from the scorecard.\\n- Keep one evidence index per gate family:\\n  - boundary reconciliation evidence;\\n  - trusted live eval evidence;\\n  - security and adversarial hardening evidence;\\n  - routing quality uplift evidence;\\n  - governance scorecard evidence.\\n- Canonical artifact paths for auditability:\\n  - `docs/reference/skill-authoring-family-boundary-decision.md`\\n  - `docs/reference/skill-authoring-family-quality-baseline.md`\\n  - `docs/reference/skill-authoring-family-risk-acceptance.md` (if needed)\\n  - `docs/reference/skill-authoring-family-gold-scorecard.md`\\n- Execution-ledger discipline:\\n  - set a phase to `in_progress` only after first implementation commit or first phase-specific validation artifact is recorded;\\n  - otherwise keep the phase `pending`.\\n- If a blocker represents durable work, route it as a durable issue with explicit evidence pointers.\\n\\n## Execution Ledger (Planning Mode)\\n\\nSTEP_ID | status (pending|in_progress|completed) | owner | evidence\\nP0 | completed | codex | References](#sources-and-references)\\n\\n## Overview\\n\\nClose the remaining gap between \\\"contract-complete\\\" and \\\"gold industry standard\\\" for the skill-authoring family:\\n\\n- `Skills/skill-builder`\\n- `Skills/skill-creator`\\n- `Skills/skill-installer`\\n- `Skills/plugin-creator`\\n\\nThis plan upgrades the family from structural pass posture into release-grade posture by:\\n\\n- reconciling family-boundary drift across specs, reference docs, and validators;\\n- making trusted live eval evidence mandatory for release readiness;\\n- eliminating outstanding security warnings in family tooling;\\n- expanding adversarial coverage beyond minimum PI checks;\\n- improving routing and execution quality signals in lower-scoring family skills;\\n- adding durable scorecard, governance, and freshness checks aligned to official documentation.\\n\\nPlan mode: `standard-plan`  \\nPlan depth: `deep`  \\nExecution posture: contract-first, security-first, live-evidence-required.\\n\\n## Problem Frame\\n\\nThe family currently passes structural gates, but several readiness gaps remain before it can be treated as \\\"gold standard\\\":\\n\\n- active enforcement scripts use `plugin-creator`, while core spec/reference text still mixes in `plugin-builder` as a family member;\\n- live runner checks are still optional in default family validation mode;\\n- `skill-builder` script surfaces still emit potential exfiltration warnings under OpenClaw;\\n- adversarial eval coverage is present but still minimum-threshold rather than broad threat-surface coverage;\\n- quality analyzer signals for `skill-installer` and `plugin-creator` show weak variation and empowerment guidance, and `plugin-creator` still has scope-focus risk;\\n- there is no single recurring scorecard process that proves continued alignment with official platform guidance as standards evolve.\\n\\nWithout a focused final hardening pass, the family can appear complete while still carrying avoidable routing, security, and governance risk.\\n\\n## Requirements Trace\\n\\n- R1. One canonical family boundary must be consistent across active scripts, specs, and references.\\n  Trace: user direction for family members; existing family gate target list.\\n- R2. Trusted live smoke and release eval evidence must be required for release-grade readiness.\\n  Trace: family-gate trusted-lane controls; maturity matrix closeout expectations.\\n- R3. Security analyzer warnings in family-critical scripts must be reduced to zero or explicitly risk-accepted with compensating controls.\\n  Trace: OpenClaw output from `Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/validate_skill_authoring_family.sh`.\\n- R4. Adversarial eval coverage must include broader threat classes beyond minimum PI command-guard checks.\\n  Trace: benchmark validator requirements + modern LLM security guidance.\\n- R5. Family SKILL routing and operator guidance must be strong enough to avoid scope confusion and weak handoffs.\\n  Trace: `analyze_skill.py` score findings for family members.\\n- R6. Ongoing official-doc alignment must be measurable and repeatable.\\n  Trace: user requirement for latest-method alignment.\\n- R7. PM and knowledge operations must include explicit readiness metrics and durable follow-up routing.\\n  Trace: repo governance pattern for durable findings and validation-led closeout.\\n\\n## Scope Boundaries\\n\\nIn scope:\\n\\n- family contract docs and references:\\n  - `Docs/specs/2026-04-03-feat-skill-authoring-family-contract-spec.md`\\n  - Treat `skill-builder`, `skill-creator`, `skill-installer`, `plugin-creator` as the active implementation family for this upgrade pass.\\n  - Rationale: this matches current validator enforcement and the requested execution target.\\n- Decision 2: Keep `plugin-builder` as an adjacent packaging surface, not an active member of this family gate.\\n  - Rationale: preserves compatibility while removing current boundary ambiguity.\\n- Decision 2a: Use a two-layer parity model for authoring and enhancement lanes.\\n  - First-layer scaffolding owners: `skill-creator` and `plugin-creator`.\\n  - Enhancement-layer owners: `skill-builder` and `plugin-builder`.\\n  - Rationale: this keeps plugin and skill workflows mentally parallel while preserving current gate ownership boundaries.\\n- Decision 3: Make trusted live smoke plus release evals required in release-ready mode.\\n  - Rationale: structural listing alone is insufficient for \\\"gold\\\" readiness claims.\\n- Decision 4: Enforce \\\"no unresolved security warning\\\" policy for family-critical scripts unless an explicit risk-acceptance record exists.\\n  - Rationale: prevents silent drift from security quality expectations.\\n- Decision 5: Use additive hardening of current scripts and docs instead of introducing a new harness.\\n  - Rationale: minimizes migration risk and keeps closeout auditable.\\n\\n## Open Questions\\n\\n### Resolved During Planning\\n\\n- Should this be a new follow-on plan or a rewrite of completed April 4 plans?\\n  - Resolution: new follow-on plan, preserving prior completion evidence and minimizing history churn.\\n- Should live-eval enforcement replace structural mode completely?\\n  - Resolution: keep structural mode for local fast checks; require trusted live mode for release-grade readiness.\\n\\n### Deferred to Implementation\\n\\n- Exact CI policy for when live-runner flakiness should auto-rerun versus fail fast.\\n  - Defer to implementation so actual runner behavior data can tune thresholds.\\n- Whether quarterly recalibration should adjust uplift deltas once the first two governance scorecard cycles complete.\\n  - Defer until two complete cycles confirm stability or reveal threshold noise.\\n\\n## High-Level Technical Design\\n\\n> This section is directional planning guidance, not implementation code.\\n\\n```mermaid\\nflowchart TD\\n SKILL_FAMILY_LIVE_EVALS_TRUSTED=1 bash Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/validate_skill_authoring_family.sh`\\n\\n**Approach:**\\n\\n- Introduce explicit release-ready mode that requires days at closeout time unless an explicit exception is approved and recorded in the scorecard.\\n- Require execution proof, not documentation-only readiness:\\n  - at least one trusted run covering smoke and release for each family skill, or\\n  - a documented split-run matrix that still proves full family coverage with retained artifacts.\\n- Define degraded-evidence handling: allow retry-limited reruns for transient runner failures, but block closeout until trusted live evidence is present.\\n\\n**Test scenarios:**\\n\\n- non-trusted live invocation fails with clear guidance;\\n- trusted invocation runs both smoke and release for each family skill;\\n- release-ready closeout blocks if live evidence is missing.\\n- artifact index includes run timestamps, skill coverage, and pass/fail outcomes for trusted runs.\\n- stale trusted artifacts (older than freshness window or from non-descendant commits) are rejected at gate time.\\n\\n**Verification:**\\n\\n- release readiness check cannot pass without trusted live eval artifacts.\\n- retry-limited live-runner failures are documented as operational noise only when a successful trusted rerun exists for the same scope.\\n- gate progression from `P1` to `P2` is blocked unless trusted execution proof is archived.\\n- trusted evidence metadata includes branch, commit SHA, run timestamp, and artifact path for freshness and lineage checks.\\n\\n**Exit criteria:**\\n\\n- \\\"gold readiness\\\" language is formally tied to trusted live execution evidence.\\n- at least one trusted live evidence set is linked from the operational scorecard path.\\n- all linked trusted evidence satisfies freshness and branch-lineage constraints.\\n\\n- [ ] **P2 / Security and Adversarial Hardening**\\n\\n**Goal:** Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/validate_skill_authoring_family.sh`\\n\\n**Approach:**\\n\\n- eliminate or guard flagged file-read/network-send patterns with explicit allowlist and output, or explicit documented risk-acceptance artifact with owner and expiration.\\n- any residual acceptance entries reference compensating controls and a removal date.\\n\\n- [ ] **P3 / Routing and Guidance Quality Uplift**\\n\\n**Goal:** Raise operator-facing quality for `skill-installer` and `plugin-creator` while preserving strict role boundaries.\\n\\n**Requirements:** R5\\n\\n**Dependencies:** P0, P2\\n\\n**Files:**\\n\\n- Modify: `Skills/skill-installer/SKILL.md`\\n- Modify: `Skills/plugin-creator/SKILL.md`\\n- Modify: `Skills/skill-creator/SKILL.md` (only if handoff clarity requires parity edits)\\n- Modify: each associated `agents/openai.yaml`\\n- Create: `docs/reference/skill-authoring-family-quality-baseline.md`\\n- Test: `~/.venvs/pyyaml/bin/python Skills/skill-builder/Infrastructure/scripts/analyze_skill.py Skills/skill-installer --min-pass 60 --no-emoji`\\n- Test: `~/.venvs/pyyaml/bin/python Skills/skill-builder/Infrastructure/scripts/analyze_skill.py Skills/plugin-creator --min-pass 60 --no-emoji`\\n- Test: `bash Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/validate_skill_authoring_family.sh`\\n\\n**Approach:**\\n\\n- improve variation-rich triggers and realistic user prompt coverage;\\n- tighten first-pass scope language to reduce cross-family confusion;\\n- improve empowerment and next-step guidance while retaining strict safety boundaries.\\n- enforce a hybrid threshold policy in `docs/reference/skill-authoring-family-quality-baseline.md`:\\n  - absolute floors:\\n    - `skill-installer`: variation >= `8/15`, empowerment >= `3/5`;\\n    - `plugin-creator`: variation >= `8/15`, empowerment >= `3/5`, scope focus >= `12/15`;\\n  - required uplift delta from baseline run:\\n    - `skill-installer`: variation delta >= `+2`, empowerment delta >= `+1`;\\n    - `plugin-creator`: variation delta >= `+2`, empowerment delta >= `+1`, scope focus delta >= `+1`;\\n  - no-regression guardrails:\\n    - no decrease in overall analyzer score from baseline;\\n    - no decrease greater than `1` point in any non-targeted analyzer subscore.\\n  - if baseline evidence is missing, `P3` cannot pass and must be treated as blocked until baseline is captured.\\n- freeze baseline immutability in the quality-baseline artifact:\\n  - record baseline branch, commit SHA, command lines, run IDs, and artifact paths;\\n  - record one baseline-freeze timestamp and prohibit mid-pass baseline replacement without an explicit change note linked from the scorecard.\\n\\n**Test scenarios:**\\n\\n- improved analyzer subscores for variation, empowerment, and scope focus;\\n- no new role-overlap regressions in routing language.\\n- baseline-to-target comparison can be audited from one document without replaying historical logs.\\n- baseline metadata is complete enough to reproduce the exact baseline run definition.\\n\\n**Verification:**\\n\\n- analyzer and eval outputs confirm clearer routing and stronger operator confidence signals.\\n- hybrid threshold checks (floor + delta + no-regression) are explicitly recorded in the quality baseline artifact.\\n- baseline lineage and freeze metadata are present and unchanged during the `P3` execution window unless Commands](#validation-commands)\\n- [Technical Review Deltas (2026-03-04)](#technical-review-deltas-2026-03-04)\\n- [Sources & References](#sources--references)\\n\\n## Overview\\nBuild a v1 intent-first skill router that returns deterministic top-3 skill recommendations with confidence and rationale, optimized for both humans and AI coding agents (see brainstorm: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`).\\n\\nThe v1 surface is CLI/report-first (no heavy UI), with rule-first deterministic scoring and auditable outputs (see brainstorm: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`).\\n\\n## Problem Statement / Motivation\\nToday, skill selection depends heavily on prompt phrasing and manual judgment. This increases misroutes, slows starts, and creates avoidable correction loops. The repository already has strong skill metadata and governance mechanisms, but lacks a dedicated routing layer for first-choice quality.\\n\\nPrimary motivation carried forward from brainstorm: improve **first-hit routing accuracy** while preserving deterministic behavior and governance controls (see brainstorm: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`).\\n\\n## Research Summary\\n### Brainstorm origin (primary input)\\n- Chosen approach: deterministic scorecard router.\\n- Rejected alternatives (for v1):\\n  - deterministic + confirm-step as default,\\n  - hybrid rules+LLM tie-break.\\n- Scope constraints:\\n  - top-3 + confidence + rationale output contract,\\n  - optimize for both humans and agents,\\n  - CLI/report-first delivery.\\n\\n(All above carried forward from: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`.)\\n\\n### Local repo findings\\n- Skill system is canonicalized around `SKILL.md` plus flat symlink sync flow (`README.md:176-180`, `Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/sync_skills.sh:126`, `Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/sync_skills.sh:158`).\\n- Existing recursive loop explicitly models routing concepts and router execution mode (`docs/skill-graphs/index.md:10`).\\n- Genome loop already computes routing confusion and candidate confidence controls (`Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/run_skill_genome_loop.py:241`, `Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/run_skill_genome_loop.py:391`, `Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/run_skill_genome_loop.py:37-39`).\\n- Existing runbook defines candidate fields and fail-closed redaction posture (`docs/skill-graphs/runbooks/skill-genome-loop.md:92-115`, `docs/skill-graphs/runbooks/skill-genome-loop.md:147`).\\n- AGENTS requirements emphasize explicit tooling, discovery order, and docs TOC (`AGENTS.md:74`, `AGENTS.md:101`, `AGENTS.md:14`).\\n\\n### Institutional learnings (closest equivalent)\\n- No `docs/solutions/` entries were found for this topic.\\n- Closest internal equivalents:\\n  - `docs/brainstorms/2026-02-24-skill-graph-live-auto-learning-brainstorm.md`\\n  - `Docs/plans/2026-02-24-feat-skill-graph-live-auto-learning-plan.md`\\n  - `docs/skill-graphs/runbooks/skill-genome-loop.md`\\n  - `docs/skill-graphs/knowledge-graph-operating-model.md`\\n\\n### External research decision\\nNo external research performed. Reason: low external-risk domain, strong internal patterns already present, and clear origin decisions from the brainstorm.\\n\\n### SpecFlow analysis highlights incorporated\\n- Define deterministic tie-break contract.\\n- Define confidence bands and machine-readable schema.\\n- Define low/zero-confidence fallback behavior.\\n- Define actor-specific first-hit instrumentation.\\n- Define non-interactive execution policy for agents.\\n- Add adversarial/abuse fixtures and telemetry redaction invariants.\\n\\n## Proposed Solution\\nCreate a scorecard-based router layer that evaluates each skill candidate using deterministic rules and outputs ranked top-3 recommendations.\\n\\n### Core v1 behavior\\n1. Normalize input request.\\n2. Load active skill catalog from canonical source.\\n3. Apply deterministic feature scoring (trigger overlap, explicit mention, anti-trigger penalties, context alignment).\\n4. Stable sort with deterministic tie-breakers.\\n5. Return top-3 with confidence + rationale in both human and JSON views.\\n6. Emit routing telemetry for first-hit KPI tracking.\\n\\n### Why this approach\\nThis is the smallest useful slice that preserves auditability and governance while improving routing quality (see brainstorm: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`). It avoids premature model dependency and keeps failure modes explicit.\\n\\n## Alternative Approaches Considered\\n1. **Deterministic + mandatory confirm step**\\n   - Benefit: lower accidental auto-selection risk.\\n   - Rejection for v1: adds interaction overhead before baseline signal is established (see brainstorm: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`).\\n2. **Hybrid rules + LLM tie-breaker**\\n   - Benefit: potentially stronger handling for highly ambiguous requests.\\n   - Rejection for v1: increased complexity, lower auditability, and harder deterministic testing (see brainstorm: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`).\\n\\n## Premortem (Failure Scenario: 2026-09-04)\\nSix months later, the router rollout is considered a failure. First-hit accuracy improved on paper but trust declined. Users bypassed suggestions, agents misrouted high-confidence tasks, and maintainers disabled active mode.\\n\\n### What went wrong\\n- Confidence scores were overfit and poorly calibrated; \\u201chigh confidence\\u201d often meant \\u201cmost keyword overlap,\\u201d not true intent.\\n- Skill catalog drifted (stale sync, inconsistent metadata quality), so deterministic ranking was deterministically wrong.\\n- Ambiguous and multi-intent prompts were forced into single-intent ranking without strong uncertainty handling.\\n- Human and agent execution policies diverged in practice; agent consumers auto-ran risky suggestions.\\n- Metrics were gameable (top-1 acceptance rose because UI nudged top candidate).\\n- Telemetry quality was noisy/incomplete, reducing ability to detect regressions early.\\n\\n### False assumptions\\n- Assumed skill descriptions/triggers were sufficiently clean for reliable scoring.\\n- Assumed deterministic output alone would create user trust.\\n- Assumed low-confidence fallback states were rare.\\n- Assumed one KPI (first-hit) captured real quality and user value.\\n\\n### Edge cases missed\\n- Negation prompts (\\u201cdon\\u2019t use X\\u201d), mixed intents, and comparative asks.\\n- Typos, alias drift, deprecated skills, and synonym gaps.\\n- Long prompts with conflicting cues and non-English phrasing.\\n- Requests mentioning dangerous capabilities where auto-run should never trigger.\\n\\n### Integration issues overlooked\\n- Catalog version mismatch between router and consumer runtimes.\\n- Incomplete contract tests across CLI, JSON consumers, and agent orchestration.\\n- Missing strong gates for rollout-state propagation and rollback drills.\\n\\n### What users hated\\n- Opaque rationales (\\u201cmatched keywords\\u201d) that felt generic.\\n- Confidently wrong top suggestion appearing repeatedly.\\n- Too much friction on uncertain prompts and too little safety on risky ones.\\n\\n## Plan Revisions from Premortem\\nTo reduce these failure modes, this plan now adds:\\n1. **Calibration-first rollout:** mandatory baseline dataset + calibration checks before active mode.\\n2. **Quality-gated metadata:** catalog freshness and SKILL metadata quality checks as preconditions.\\n3. **Uncertainty and safety-first policy:** explicit no-auto-run rules for risky/ambiguous cases.\\n4. **Anti-gaming metrics:** add override regret and correction-latency metrics, not just top-1 acceptance.\\n5. **Cross-surface contract testing:** strict roundtrip tests for CLI, JSON, and agent consumers.\\n6. **Operational resilience:** hard control precedence, rollback drills, and auto-downgrade triggers.\\n\\n## Technical Considerations\\n- **Architecture impacts**\\n  - Introduce a routing contract layer separate from existing skill definitions.\\n  - Reuse current metadata pipeline (`SKILL.md` index and sync artifacts) rather than adding a second skill registry.\\n  - Keep rollout controls compatible with existing `off | observe_only | active` posture.\\n\\n- **Performance implications**\\n  - Target low-latency local ranking for interactive CLI usage.\\n  - Use precomputed skill metadata snapshot where possible.\\n  - Keep deterministic sort and scoring O(n) or O(n log n) over skill count.\\n\\n- **Security considerations**\\n  - Do not persist raw sensitive prompt text in routing logs.\\n  - Enforce telemetry allowlist + redaction-at-write.\\n  - Ensure non-interactive mode cannot silently auto-run low-confidence routes.\\n  - Default `agent_mode=observe_only` until rollout gates pass.\\n\\n## System-Wide Impact\\n- **Interaction graph**\\n  - Request ingestion triggers router scoring, which produces ranked candidates, then selected skill execution, then telemetry logging.\\n  - In observe-only mode, execution behavior remains unchanged; only recommendation + telemetry layers are added.\\n\\n- **Error propagation**\\n  - Catalog load or schema errors must return machine-readable error payloads and non-zero exit.\\n  - Low-confidence is not an error; it is an explicit routing state with fallback guidance.\\n  - Invalid controls (missing/unreadable/malformed) fail closed to safe mode (`observe_only` or `off`).\\n\\n- **State lifecycle risks**\\n  - Risk: stale skill index causing poor recommendations.\\n  - Mitigation: include Infrastructure/catalog/version metadata in each router result and block active mode when freshness checks fail.\\n\\n- **API surface parity**\\n  - Human CLI report and JSON output must reflect identical ranking and confidence values.\\n  - Agent consumers must rely on the same schema version used by CLI JSON output.\\n\\n- **Integration test scenarios**\\n  1. Ambiguous request with two near-equal candidates yields stable order across repeated runs.\\n  2. Explicit skill mention always outranks generic intent-only matches.\\n  3. Non-interactive low-confidence request returns `requires_clarification=true` and no implicit auto-run.\\n  4. Broken skill metadata entry is skipped/flagged without crashing full ranking.\\n  5. First-hit telemetry records rank-chosen and actor type correctly.\\n  6. Multi-intent and negation prompts produce uncertainty flags instead of false-confidence routing.\\n\\n## Implementation Phases\\n### Phase 1: Contract + control foundation\\n- Define invocation boundary contract (input shape, actor type, policy mode, output/error contract).\\n- Define router output schema v1 (human + JSON parity).\\n- Define telemetry allowlist/redaction contract with forbidden fields.\\n- Define deterministic scoring/tie-break specification and confidence bands.\\n- Define rollout control precedence (`kill-switch > rollback-required > rollout-mode`) with fail-closed behavior.\\n\\n### Phase 2: Data quality + calibration foundation\\n- Build benchmark fixture set (clear, ambiguous, adversarial, multi-intent, negation, deprecated aliases).\\n- Define calibration policy (threshold bands + acceptable error rates by actor type).\\n- Add catalog freshness/version checks tied to sync pipeline.\\n- Add metadata quality checks (required fields, trigger completeness, anti-trigger presence where applicable).\\n\\n### Phase 3: Router execution path\\n- Implement input normalization and candidate scoring.\\n- Implement deterministic ranking and rationale generation.\\n- Add CLI/report rendering and JSON output mode.\\n- Add explicit uncertainty handling for ambiguous/multi-intent prompts.\\n\\n### Phase 4: Metrics + controlled rollout\\n- Add first-hit instrumentation by actor type (`human|agent`).\\n- Add anti-gaming metrics (override regret, correction latency, repeat-misroute rate).\\n- Add observe-only rollout baseline window and go/no-go thresholds.\\n- Add rollback drill and kill-switch propagation verification before active mode.\\n- Add auto-downgrade triggers back to `observe_only` on safety/quality regressions.\\n\\n## Planned File Map\\n- `Skills/skill-builder/Infrastructure/scripts/skill_router.py` (new router engine)\\n- `Skills/skill-builder/Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/skill_catalog.py` (canonical catalog loader + quality checks)\\n- `Skills/skill-builder/Infrastructure/scripts/skill_router_schema.py` (schema + confidence contract)\\n- `Skills/skill-builder/Infrastructure/scripts/test_skill_router.py` (determinism + edge-case tests)\\n- `Skills/skill-builder/Infrastructure/scripts/test_skill_router_fixtures.json` (ranking/adversarial fixtures)\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/verify_router_schema.py` (schema/telemetry contract verifier)\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/validation-and-linting/verify_skill_catalog_freshness.py` (catalog freshness and metadata quality gates)\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/skill_router_metrics.py` (first-hit + guardrail KPI aggregation)\\n- `Infrastructure/scripts/lifecycle-and-sync/run_skill_router_rollback_drill.sh` (control-precedence rollback drill)\\n- `docs/skill-graphs/schemas/skill-router.schema.md` (versioned contract)\\n- `docs/skill-graphs/runbooks/skill-router.md` (operational runbook)\\n- `docs/skill-graphs/telemetry/daily-skill-health.md` (metric additions)\\n- `docs/skill-graphs/telemetry/skill-router-go-no-go-thresholds.md` (rollout go/no-go thresholds)\\n\\n## Task Graph (id / depends_on)\\n```yaml\\ntasks:\\n  - id: T0\\n    title: override regret decreases,\\n  - correction latency decreases,\\n  - repeat misroute rate decreases,\\n  - low-confidence false auto-run rate approaches zero,\\n  - telemetry redaction violation rate remains zero.\\n\\n## Dependencies & Risks\\n### Dependencies\\n- Canonical skill metadata consistency (`SKILL.md`, sync outputs, and skill folders).\\n- Existing telemetry artifact paths for routing outcomes.\\n- Rollout-control conventions already used in skill-graph runbooks.\\n\\n### Risks and mitigations\\n- **Underspecified confidence semantics** \\u2192 lock versioned confidence-band contract and calibration thresholds.\\n- **Tie instability across environments** \\u2192 deterministic tie-break order and golden fixture tests.\\n- **Overfitting score weights** \\u2192 benchmark + calibration window before active mode.\\n- **Telemetry sensitivity leakage** \\u2192 allowlist + redaction-at-write + invariant tests.\\n- **Control misconfiguration** \\u2192 explicit precedence checks and fail-closed safe mode.\\n- **Catalog drift** \\u2192 freshness and metadata quality gates.\\n- **Scope creep into UI or LLM tie-breakers** \\u2192 defer until v1 KPIs/guardrails validate deterministic baseline (see brainstorm: `docs/brainstorms/2026-03-04-skill-router-brainstorm.md`).\\n\\n## Open Questions\\nFrom premortem + technical review, carry forward for implementation-resolution:\\n1. Exact numeric thresholds for confidence bands and policy gates.\\n2. Minimum baseline sample window for go/no-go active rollout.\\n3. Whether deprecated aliases are included in v1 compatibility scope.\\n4. Final list of router JSON fields needed by downstream agent orchestration.\\n\\n## AI-Era Delivery Considerations\\n- Keep router outputs easily consumable by both humans and agents from day one.\\n- Require explicit test fixtures for ambiguous/adversarial prompts to prevent regression from rapid iteration.\\n- Treat auto-routing behavior as high-impact; require human review for threshold/policy changes.\\n- Document generated scoring heuristics and keep rationale traceable to stable rule identifiers.\\n\\n## Validation Commands\\n- `python3 ~/.codex/Infrastructure/scripts/plan-graph-lint.py
